patch(\"source.task_handlers.travel.guide_builder._create_ltx_control_guide_video\", return_value=guide_path):\n+        with patch(\"source.task_handlers.travel.segment_processor.get_video_frame_count_and_fps\", return_value=(17, 16)):\n+            result = processor.process_segment()\n+\n+    assert result[\"audio_prompt_type\"] == \"K\"\n+    assert result[\"audio_guide\"] is None\n+\n+\n+def test_ic_lora_uses_control_strength_for_hybrid_and_strength_for_legacy(tmp_path: Path):\n+    hybrid = TravelGuidanceConfig.from_payload(\n+        {\n+            \"travel_guidance\": _hybrid_payload(\n+                anchors=[{\"image_url\": _write_image(tmp_path / \"a.png\"), \"frame_position\": 0}],\n+                videos=[VIDEO_ENTRY],\n+                mode=\"pose\",\n+                control_strength=0.33,\n+                strength=0.05,\n+            )\n+        },\n+        \"ltx2_22B_distilled\",\n+    )\n+    legacy = TravelGuidanceConfig.from_payload(\n+        {\"travel_guidance\": {\"kind\": \"ltx_control\", \"mode\": \"pose\", \"videos\": [VIDEO_ENTRY], \"strength\": 0.8}},\n+        \"ltx2_22B_distilled\",\n+    )\n+\n+    hybrid_loras = _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora([], hybrid)\n+    legacy_loras = _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora([], legacy)\n+\n+    assert hybrid_loras == [\n+        {\n+            \"path\": IC_LORA_UNION_CONTROL_FILENAME,\n+            \"strength\": 0.33,\n+            \"name\": \"ic-lora-union-control (auto-injected)\",\n+        }\n+    ]\n+    assert legacy_loras[0][\"strength\"] == 0.8\n+\n+\n+def test_build_generation_params_wires_hybrid_fields():\n+    ctx = SegmentContext(\n+        mode=\"orchestrator\",\n+        orchestrator_details={},\n+        individual_params={},\n+        segment_idx=0,\n+        segment_params={},\n+    )\n+    gen = GenerationInputs(\n+        model_name=\"ltx2_22B_distilled\",\n+        prompt_for_wgp=\"prompt\",\n+        negative_prompt_for_wgp=\"\",\n+        parsed_res_wh=(64, 64),\n+        total_frames_for_segment=17,\n+        current_run_base_output_dir=Path(\"/tmp\"),\n+        segment_processing_dir=Path(\"/tmp\"),\n+        debug_enabled=False,\n+        travel_mode=\"ltx\",\n+    )\n+    structure = StructureOutputs(\n+        guide_video_path=\"/tmp/guide.mp4\",\n+        video_prompt_type_str=\"PVGKFI\",\n+        structure_config=TravelGuidanceConfig(kind=\"none\"),\n+        image_refs_paths=[\"/tmp/a.png\"],\n+        frames_positions=\"1\",\n+        image_refs_strengths=[0.7],\n+        audio_guide=\"/tmp/audio.wav\",\n+        audio_prompt_type=\"A\",\n+        audio_scale=0.4,\n+        denoising_strength=0.6,\n+    )\n+\n+    generation_params = _build_generation_params(ctx, gen, ImageRefs(), structure, \"task-1\")\n+\n+    assert generation_params[\"image_refs_paths\"] == [\"/tmp/a.png\"]\n+    assert generation_params[\"frames_positions\"] == \"1\"\n+    assert generation_params[\"image_refs_strengths\"] == [0.7]\n+    assert generation_params[\"audio_guide\"] == \"/tmp/audio.wav\"\n+    assert generation_params[\"audio_prompt_type\"] == \"A\"\n+    assert generation_params[\"audio_scale\"] == 0.4\n+    assert generation_params[\"denoising_strength\"] == 0.6\n+\n+\n+def test_non_travel_task_conversion_whitelists_new_fields(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):\n+    class DummyQwenHandler:\n+        def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):\n+            pass\n+\n+    monkeypatch.setattr(\n+        \"source.task_handlers.tasks.task_conversion.QwenHandler\",\n+        DummyQwenHandler,\n+    )\n+\n+    task = db_task_to_generation_task(\n+        {\n+            \"prompt\": \"prompt\",\n+            \"model\": \"ltx2_22B_distilled\",\n+            \"input_video_strength\": 0.9,\n+            \"image_refs_strengths\": [0.2, 0.8],\n+            \"audio_scale\": 0.6,\n+        },\n+        task_id=\"task-1\",\n+        task_type=\"z_image_turbo\",\n+        wan2gp_path=\"/tmp\",\n+    )\n+\n+    assert task.parameters[\"input_video_strength\"] == 0.9\n+    assert task.parameters[\"image_refs_strengths\"] == [0.2, 0.8]\n+    assert task.parameters[\"audio_scale\"] == 0.6\n*** End Patch\n"
}

The main changes are in [travel_guidance.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/core/params/travel_guidance.py), [orchestrator.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py), [segment_processor.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/travel/segment_processor.py), [task_registry.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py), and the vendored WGP/LTX files [wgp.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/Wan2GP/wgp.py) and [ltx2.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py). time.monotonic())))\n+\n+        raise queue.Empty\n \n     def get_task_status(self, task_id: str) -> Optional[GenerationTask]:\n         \"\"\"Get status of a specific task.\"\"\"\n         return self.task_history.get(task_id)\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/media/video/video_info.py\n@@\n \"\"\"Video metadata retrieval using ffprobe and OpenCV.\"\"\"\n+from pathlib import Path\n import json\n import subprocess\n import time\n \n import cv2\n@@\n __all__ = [\n+    \"TimeoutExpired\",\n     \"VideoMetadataError\",\n     \"get_video_frame_count_ffprobe\",\n     \"get_video_fps_ffprobe\",\n     \"get_video_frame_count_and_fps\",\n+    \"require_video_frame_count_and_fps\",\n+    \"run_subprocess\",\n ]\n+\n+run_subprocess = subprocess.run\n+TimeoutExpired = subprocess.TimeoutExpired\n@@\n-        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n+        result = run_subprocess(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n@@\n-        result2 = subprocess.run(cmd2, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)\n+        result2 = run_subprocess(cmd2, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)\n@@\n-    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n+    except TimeoutExpired:\n         generation_logger.warning(f\"[FFPROBE] Timeout getting frame count for {input_video_path}\")\n         return None\n@@\n-        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n+        result = run_subprocess(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n@@\n-    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n+    except TimeoutExpired:\n         generation_logger.warning(f\"[FFPROBE] Timeout getting FPS for {input_video_path}\")\n         return None\n@@\n-    # If all attempts failed, return what we got (might be 0 or invalid)\n-    return frames, fps\n+    ffprobe_frames = get_video_frame_count_ffprobe(input_video_path)\n+    ffprobe_fps = get_video_fps_ffprobe(input_video_path)\n+\n+    resolved_frames = frames if frames and frames > 0 else ffprobe_frames\n+    resolved_fps = fps if fps and fps > 0 else ffprobe_fps\n+\n+    return resolved_frames, resolved_fps\n+\n+\n+def require_video_frame_count_and_fps(\n+    input_video_path: str | Path,\n+    *,\n+    context: str = \"video metadata\",\n+) -> tuple[int, float]:\n+    \"\"\"Return required frame-count/FPS metadata or raise a structured error.\"\"\"\n+    frame_count, fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(input_video_path))\n+    if not frame_count or frame_count <= 0 or not fps or fps <= 0:\n+        raise VideoMetadataError(f\"{context}: could not determine frame count\")\n+    return int(frame_count), float(fps)\n*** End Patch\n"
}

local_file_path.exists():\n+        return None\n+\n+    request = resolve_edge_request(\"generate-upload-url\", runtime_config=runtime_config)\n+    if not request.url:\n+        return None\n+    if not has_required_edge_credentials(request.headers):\n+        return None\n+\n+    content_type = mimetypes.guess_type(str(local_file_path))[0] or \"application/octet-stream\"\n+    response, > AGENT

1. `image_refs_strengths` is still silently dropped before the real WGP call. [task_registry.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py):859 does emit the field, but [wgp_params.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py):312 and [wgp_params.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py):390 build the allowed WGP kwarg set without `image_refs_strengths`, and [wgp_params.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py):438 drops unknown kwargs. The headless path therefore never reaches the vendored `generate_video(..., image_refs_strengths=...)` change. This breaks rule 16 end-to-end, and it means per-anchor strengths from the plan are not actually honored.

2. Multi-window anchor alignment is still wrong in vendored WGP. [wgp.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/Wan2GP/wgp.py):6425 filters window-local frame indices and strengths, but [wgp.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/Wan2GP/wgp.py):6670 still passes the full `src_ref_images` list. Then [ltx2.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx2.py):973 zips `input_ref_images` against the filtered `frames_relative_positions_list`. On later windows, the wrong image can be paired with a later position/strength. That violates rule 4’s alignment invariant.

3. A hybrid segment can emit `audio_prompt_type="K"` without any `V` in `video_prompt_type`. [orchestrator.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py):208 clones hybrid configs and clears `videos` when control no longer overlaps a segment, but it keeps `audio`. [segment_processor.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/travel/segment_processor.py):191 emits `"K"` whenever `audio.source == "control_track"`, while [segment_processor.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/travel/segment_processor.py):225 only adds `VG/PVG/DVG/EVG` when `has_control` is still true. Result: anchors-only segment payloads can become `KFI` + `K`, which violates rule 8.

4. The contract implements rule 2 more strictly than the plan and rejects valid inputs. [travel_guidance.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/core/params/travel_guidance.py):383 rejects any repeated global `frame_position`, while the hard rules say global duplicates are allowed and only duplicate local positions after remap should fail. [test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py):139 locks that stricter behavior in as expected behavior.

5. Preflight strict mode still has one silent-skip path. [preflight.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/models/wgp/generators/preflight.py):33 continues on falsy `image_refs_paths` entries even when strict alignment mode is active. Earlier validation should catch empty anchor URLs on the intended travel path, but rule 4 said to remove silent-skip behavior at preflight, and this path still exists.

6. The Phase 0 semantic gate test does not actually exercise the boundary it was meant to protect. [test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py):107 stops at `_build_generation_params()` plus `prepare_svi_image_refs()`, then manually calls `prepare_video_guide_and_mask` at [test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py):137. It never runs the real WGP kwarg assembly/filtering path, which is why the `image_refs_strengths` drop in item 1 was not caught.

Rules 2, 4, 8, and 16 are not correctly implemented as shipped. Rules 1, 3, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 17, and 18 looked consistent in the reviewed code. I did not find additional rule-breaking issues in [guide_builder.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/travel/guide_builder.py), [task_conversion.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_conversion.py), or [test_travel_guidance_config.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py) beyond the rule-2 mismatch already noted.

I re-ran the full sense check, fixed the gaps I found, and pushed the branch.

The follow-up fixes close the remaining hybrid travel holes in [wgp_params.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/models/wgp/generators/wgp_params.py), [wgp.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/Wan2GP/wgp.py), [travel_guidance.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/core/params/travel_guidance.py), [orchestrator.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py), [segment_processor.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/travel/segment_processor.py), and [preflight.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/models/wgp/generators/preflight.py). The main corrections were:
- `image_refs_strengths` now survives the headless WGP kwarg builder.
- Multi-window anchor images/strengths stay aligned in vendored WGP.
- `control_track` audio is dropped or rejected when a segment no longer has control overlap.
- Strict preflight now fails on empty anchor paths too.
- Hybrid duplicate-local-position enforcement now happens at segment remap time, not global contract parse time.

Verification passed:
- `python -m pytest tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py -x -q`
- `python -m pytest tests/ -x`
- Result: `681 passed, 11 warnings in 23.90s`

Pushed to GitHub:
- Branch: `feat/worker-matrix-hardening`
- Commit: `c6a08fd9` (`fix: close hybrid travel transport gaps`)

> DEVELOPER

So you 1000% confident that this will work?

> AGENT

No.

I’m confident the current branch is materially better and the automated coverage is strong, but “1000%” would be dishonest.

What I am confident about:
- The full test suite passes: `681 passed`.
- The specific rule breaks I found in the hostile review are now addressed.
- The transport path for hybrid anchors/strengths/audio is exercised better than before.

What I am not fully confident about:
- Real runtime behavior inside vendored Wan2GP with actual long multi-window LTX hybrid generations. The tests validate the logic and boundary plumbing, but they do not replace a true end-to-end generation run on real media.
- Any behavior hidden behind model-specific branches, GPU memory pressure, or vendor-side assumptions that only appear during live inference.

If you want the highest-confidence next step, I’d run one real smoke generation for:
1. `KFI` only
2. `VGKFI` or `PVGKFI`
3. A multi-window hybrid case with anchors spanning more than one window

If you want, I can set up and run those smoke checks now.

> AGENT

Reasonably well-engineered in the “battle-hardened system” sense, not in the “clean, elegant codebase” sense.

The good part is real: the branch has strong contract coverage, explicit boundary tests, and the mechanical health is high. The quick scan after this pass shows `objective 96/100`, `test health 99.9%`, and the full suite is green. That usually means regressions get caught and the system is safer to change than most codebases this size.

The weak part is also real: the same scan still puts `strict` at `77/100`, with design coherence, cross-module architecture, init coupling, and structure/navigation all notably weak. That matches what I saw directly: too many broad modules, too much coordination logic spread across layers, and vendor/runtime boundaries that are easy to get subtly wrong.

So my honest answer is: solid enough to ship and extend carefully, but not clean enough that I’d call it generally excellent engineering. I’d describe it as reliable-under-test, somewhat overgrown, and still carrying meaningful architecture debt.
